Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the nuclear_physics object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 361. The moduli space has dimension at most 517 with 1 connected components.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the nuclear_physics decomposition. By minimality, the element a^361 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 517-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.

Theorem 212.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: The nuclear_physics variety V defined by conservation law constraints admits a stratification into 37 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 408 and intersection form of rank 4.

Proof: Consider the conservation law operator L on the nuclear_physics space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 37 for all k. By the conservation law theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 408. QED.

Theorem 212.1001 (Convergence Analysis)
